- THE CORE ALGEBRA, derived and numerically verified on synthetic random bars (no market data): with the prior close inside the bar, rawDelta = volume * ((high + low) - 2*prevClose) / (high - low) (max abs error 6.4e-9 over 200k bars); THE CLOSE NEVER ENTERS THE FORMULA - the "delta" is volume-weighted candle anatomy (where the bar midpoint sits relative to the prior close), not order flow; gap bars are always signed in the gap direction, muted by gap extent (gap-up: +volume*(high-low)/(high-prevClose), error 9.1e-13); |rawDelta| <= volume. Consequences recorded: reversal bars are misread by construction (an upthrust closing on its lows prints as buying) - the exact failure mode that matters for a turning-point programme; overnight gaps mechanically pump the cumulative in the gap direction; the AVFI honesty class applies (derived geometry, no aggressive-flow classification).

# Hourly AVFI Session Log (append-only)

Protocol for the 24 scheduled hourly tasks ("AVFI edge research: tops & bottoms"):

1. Read this log's latest entries before starting, to build on the previous session's state.
2. Do one concrete improvement per session (mathematics, Pine v5 code, registered amendment, or unsealed-data test) toward an AVFI edge in detecting tops and bottoms on NQ weekly bars.
3. Never touch the sealed 2021+ holdout, never run the stage 6b battery, never edit frozen hypotheses or registered rule sets silently (dated amendments only).
4. Append a dated entry below with: what was read/changed, files touched, findings (evidence vs speculation), and the suggested next step. Never delete or rewrite prior entries.

---

2026-11-11, session 1 (manual, this conversation, Andrea directive to start the tops/bottoms work):
- Code review of the base AVFI v3 (pasted by Andrea; identical to strategies/avfi_v3_reference.pine). Findings: sp_z self-contamination (current bar inside its own mean/stdev window, biases against extreme detection); moment-based z not fat-tail robust; no divergence, climax, or break-guard logic anywhere; programme §8 CUSUM guard never implemented; stage-3 lesson (transitions at spread≈0) means top/bottom work must not key on |sp_z| magnitude.
- Built strategies/avfi_v32_topbottom.pine (v3.1 verbatim + tops/bottoms layer): robust IQR spread z with prior-bar window, wave-free 20-week divergence flags (H8 draft), effort-vs-result climax flags (H9 draft, rho>=1.5 and |z|<=0.2 at fresh extremes), CUSUM regime-break guard (k=0.5σ, h=4σ, 13-week flag hold), markers, panel rows, alerts. Core v3 logic untouched; indicator not strategy.
- Registered docs/amendment3_h8_h9_topbottom.md (draft, pending Andrea's sign-off) BEFORE any evaluation; defaults frozen (pbN 20, fadeZ 0, climRho 1.5, cusumK 0.5, cusumH 4, breakHold 13). Shipped as canvas 'AVFI v3.2 Tops/Bottoms Research Edition'.
- Next step (suggested for the next hourly session): pandas replication + unit tests of the robust-z and CUSUM layers against the Pine, then the H8/H9 unsealed (1999-2020) event studies with the pre-committed bootstrap controls. Holdout untouched.

Recommendation: do not sign Amendment 4 as drafted. - Programme-level lesson recorded: ANY correlation-style test on the smooth weekly registered series at n~300 is invalid under within-sample resampling; future hypotheses need persistence-preserving nulls or event-study machinery.

- STAGE 0e (NEW research/stage0e_artifact_occupancy.py + report): tested the one remaining principled explanation of the H8 null - roll/expiry artifact contamination of the event population - by occupancy counting only (no forward returns; session 2/3 precedent). Definition identical to stage 0d and the v3.3 Pine marker. divTop 7/32 (21.9%) artifact-adjacent vs 15.1% for the fresh-20w-high control arm and a 15.4% base rate; Fisher OR 2.16, p 0.15 one-sided (descriptive); climax events 0/6; tbFlagged 5/27. Two of my own analysis bugs caught pre-record: (a) I first filtered ALL classes to the sp_rz-valid window, which wrongly dropped climax events that never touch sp_rz (climaxTop 2 vs registered 3, climaxBot 0 vs 3) - populations must not silently mix; (b) my first Fisher table double-counted because divTop events are a subset of the newHigh control arm (corrected to a mutually exclusive comparison).
- DECISION (recorded, no Andrea action needed): H8b (artifact-exclusion refinement of H8) NOT DRAFTED. Weak concentration, 25/32 events already clean, and the arithmetic bound from published stage2b aggregates (no new forward returns) says a flip needs the 7 artifact events to average ~+1.31 sigma above control at 4 weeks vs the observed population mean +0.17. Drafting it would spend multiplicity on a hypothesis with no grounding. The artifact-contamination explanation of the H8 null is closed. [Later note, added at restoration time: this conclusion is unaffected by everything sessions 8-13 subsequently built.]
- GENUINE PARITY BUG FOUND AND FIXED: Pine sd_s = ta.stdev(spread, normWin) uses Pine's DEFAULT biased/population stdev (ddof=0) - a fact the programme's own v3.1 overlay comment states - while the validated pandas reference pine_stdev is SAMPLE stdev (ddof=1), the convention every registered study ran on. The v3/v3.1/v3.2/v3.2.1/v3.3 chart has therefore been ~0.1% (sqrt(500/499)) off the registered series on sd_s/sp_z/CUSUM scale. Quantified in pandas BEFORE shipping: ZERO flag differences on the canonical 1365 bars (CUSUM alarms 62/62, nearBreak 329/329, tbFlagged 27/27 unsealed, divTop/climax populations identical); displayed sp_z/bands shift by at most 0.0042.
- v3.3.1 (NEW strategies/avfi_v331_research.pine; avfi_v33_research.pine stays untouched as shipped): sd_s = ta.stdev(spread, normWin, false). My first draft (manual sum((x-mu_s)^2)/(n-1), mirroring the vol8 fix) was REJECTED in validation before shipping: its na-poisoning delays sd_s validity to bar ~1258 vs the reference's 759 (math.sum needs normWin consecutive valid bars of spread-mu_s), which would have silently shifted CUSUM resets across the entire early window - the "unexecuted code is unvalidated code" lesson again, caught by translating the Pine line to pandas and comparing na patterns. The native biased=false argument preserves the original line's exact na semantics. Doc-only convention note added to avfi_replication.py. [Later note, added at restoration time: sessions 8-13 confirmed the fix is inherited into v3.4 and v3.5 (both sd_s and the new sd_c) and the finding is cited in-file as "pandas parity per v3.3.1 finding".]
